### p200m_4b_0902 — OOM at launch (b32a1, 93.8 GiB)
- **Asked:** 200M@4B 在 e19eeb7 的启动行下 tok/s/gpu >= 70K（facts/efficiency.json#eff.fb_mfu 的 73K 是基线），首 50 步 loss 与 500M 的同步数可对读
- **Learned:** eff.microbatch_32_oom reproduced (pod row, merged 2026-09-03) batch 32 accum 1 OOM in the first backward, 93.78 GiB/card, rank3 first. Predicted by facts/efficiency.json#eff.microbatch_32_oom (2026-08-31): 'micro-batch 32 x accum 1 OOMs at seq 4096 fp8 (93.8/95.2 GB per card, ranks 3/6 first)'. Reproduced to two decimals. | the launch line carried a shape an existing fact says cannot run, and its verdict was already written: 'keep micro-batch 16; grow effective batch via accum, not micro-batch'. Two launches re-measured it. My attempt-1 diagnosis made it worse: eight foreign pids (2312358-2312365) WERE holding ~95 GiB when attempt 1 allocated, so I blamed contention and relaunched the same shape. A true observation that explains the symptom stops the search -- and the discriminator I skipped was one grep of the fact store for the shape being launched, which names the same per-card GiB and the same first ranks.
- **So:** b16a2

### prof200m_step_cost_v2 — OOM at step 0: 93.3 GiB allocated of a 95.22 GiB card, on a (131072,3072) bf16 FFN buffer; 206.13M params built, no step timed, rc 1
- **Asked:** 在 200M@4B 配置下，主机侧每步成本的四项分解各是多少毫秒：loader 等待、save、val、NCCL 下界；step 形状 = e19eeb7 的 p200m 启动行
- **Learned:** the profiler recorded fp8=True while calling neither of train.py:2019-2020's two fp8 steps, so the model kept fp32 parameters and every activation doubled. Batch shape was correct (32x4096, accum 1, 131072/4096 = 32 = Cfg.batch), so the OOM was the missing bf16 cast, not an oversized batch. A record describing a premise the code never established -- same shape as the EOS_ID hasattr fallback that was the only branch.
- **So:** fixed in de@a87b7ba: apply raw.to(bfloat16) + convert_to_fp8_compute, record the APPLIED fp8, drop a .long() that widened the 14.9 GiB int32 plan to 29.8 GiB of host RAM. Selftest gained an AST check of main() (a substring version could not fail -- the needle sat in the check's own table). Rerun after de merges to main.

### m4b_mix_startup — est_startup 316.05s = 4.39% of 2h run (torch.load 88.68s + corpus_fp walk 227.36s)

- **So:** if cutting startup, optimize _corpus_fp on zh_web (cache fp or fewer shards); a build_mix change

### eval_p500m_step2500_base_v3 — minimal_pairs 78.3% (numeric 60%), lambada_zh acc@1 22.3% acc@5 46.1% two-way 91.6%, C-Eval 23.0%, math_v2_like 96.7%
- **Asked:** Cache-free likelihood panel on step2500 vs step1500; a metric moving beyond its noise threshold says the run is learning
- **Learned:** vs step1500: minimal_pairs +3.2pt, lambada acc@5 +3.9pt, acc@1 +0.8, C-Eval -2.6 (chance), math_v2_like saturated; direction consistent on the two metrics with resolution, magnitudes at or inside noise; eval cost one 9K tok/s window (~200s)
- **So:** keep the base panel every 1000 steps; generative panel at run end; domain_loss needs de-26 (ckpt's own mix, sliced reads)

### eval_p500m_step2500_base_v2 — no metrics
- **Asked:** Cache-free likelihood panel on step2500 (2.62B tok) vs step1500 (1.57B tok): minimal_pairs, C-Eval, lambada_zh, math_v2_like; a metric that moves beyond its noise threshold says the run is learning; domain_loss excluded because it would torch.load the 166 GB mix_500m caches next to the live run
- **Learned:** --metrics takes space-separated names, a comma list is one unknown metric
- **So:** relaunch with spaces

### eval_p500m_step2500_base — no metrics
- **Asked:** Likelihood-only base panel on step2500 (2.62B tok) vs step1500 (1.57B tok): minimal_pairs, lambada_zh, ceval, math_v2_like move above their bootstrap CI if the run is learning; co-resident cost measured at 46s/2.3GiB by de-27, generative metrics SKIP on base
- **Learned:** cache_guard refused: web_hq .vocab stamp empty (one of the seven 0-byte 09-01 stamps); score_matrix's domain_loss reads a mix that is not the run's mix_500m
- **So:** rerun with --metrics restricted to cache-free likelihood metrics; domain_loss default mix must come from the checkpoint's cfg (de-26)

### m1b_mine_0000_v2 — 0 passed (stdlib_only 1, matched 0)
- **Asked:** fixed miner (pairing+whitelist+runner) yields runnable impl+test pairs on shard 0000
- **Learned:** strict single-blob stdlib-only join collapses ~150 loose candidates to ~0: github tests import pkg via dotted path or relative/framework, not bare sibling+stdlib
- **So:** approach not viable at scale; fb decides repo-level sandbox vs curated sources (HumanEval+/MBPP+ == 3b-9)

### m1b_mine_0000 — 0/12551 passed
- **Asked:** code_supply yields runnable impl+test pairs (stdlib-only, sandbox rc 0)
- **Learned:** single-blob stdlib-only code mining is structurally zero: test-imports point at whole-package deps, not a same-repo basename implementable in isolation
- **So:** fb ruled option B: use curated 3b-9 sets, drop github mining

### eval_p500m_step1500_ppl_v2 — no ppl numbers: killed at 07:29Z before the first domain scored (log 0 bytes -- still loading the 2.1GB checkpoint). Guard PASS 9/9 domains recorded separately, cardless.
- **Asked:** Per-domain held-out loss for p500m at step 1500, and the first live test of eval/cache_guard.py: the guard must let a fresh-cache read through (9/9 verified cardless: vocab_id 0bce3584bc24f255, .vocab/.srcfp/.seed all match) and no 'retokenizing' line may appear. The 06:13Z attempt had no guard and was two minutes from rewriting nine live training caches.
- **Learned:** The delivery is the guard, not a loss table. assert_caches_fresh returned 9 domains fresh with CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES='' -- vocab_id 0bce3584bc24f255, .vocab/.srcfp/.seed matching, cache newer than shards -- so the question the 06:13Z attempt needed two minutes on a card to reach was answered in seconds on zero cards, before launch. Throughput went 12K tok/s/gpu at step 1850 to 8K at step 1900 (MFU 8%, ETA 54h->83h) concurrent with this launch on card 7; the eval never reached a GPU op, so the contention was checkpoint load and cache IO, not compute.
- **So:** Kill by exact container PIDs (2448771/2448770/2448769), no orphan holds a card -- nvidia-smi shows exactly the 8 training ranks at 73.5GB each. ppl waits for the run to finish. The likelihood-class-may-share-a-card rule is wrong as written: it was measured on score_matrix at 2.3GiB, and ppl torch.loads a 85GB cache per domain. A sharing rule has to be written in IO and memory, not in metric class.

### eval_p500m_step1500_l1 — killed after 6 min, no output
- **Asked:** l1 few-shot on the 20B run at step 1500; run-named preds so the artifact guard does not collide with the earlier checkpoint file
- **Learned:** generative l1 few-shot on a shared block card halves training throughput (12K -> 6K tok/s/gpu) for its whole duration; likelihood metrics cost ~4 min at 2.3 GiB, generation does not fit beside the run
- **So:** no generative eval on a block card while the run trains; rerun l1 at run end or on a freed card

### eval_p500m_step1500_base — minimal_pairs 75.1% (numeric 55%, word_order 85.9%, classifier 88.1%); lambada_zh open_acc1 21.5% two_way 91.2%; C-Eval 25.6% (chance); math_v2_like 97.2% (twin likelihood, saturated)
- **Asked:** base-profile score matrix of the 20B run at step 1500 (1.57B tok) minus domain_loss (touches the live mix cache); MC suite expected near chance, minimal pairs and lambada are the readable numbers
- **Learned:** at 1.57B tokens the grammar/word-order signal is already strong, numeric minimal pairs sit at 55% (near chance), C-Eval at chance as expected for base; math_v2_like is saturated (1.0 on 8 families) so it cannot resolve progress at this step
- **So:** record only; next base eval at step 5000 or run end; ppl and domain_loss on the pod only after ppl.py sets VOCAB_ID (de-23)

### eval_p500m_step1500_ppl — killed at 2 min, no output
- **Asked:** per-domain val perplexity of the 20B run at step 1500 (1.57B tok), on a block card with 25 GiB headroom; base-appropriate metric, generative evals skip on base
- **Learned:** eval/ppl.py calls build_mix with train.VOCAB_ID unset (only train.py:1462 sets it), so every live cache reads as another vocabulary and would be retokenized and restamped under the live run
- **So:** no eval touches build_mix on the pod while a run is alive; fix ppl.py to set VOCAB_ID from the checkpoint; build_mix refuses on VOCAB_ID None (de)

### degen_t08_base — base 0-shot t=0.8: 0/497 = 0.0% (greedy also 0/497); degeneracy 25.4% -> 12.1%
- **Asked:** Control arm for degen_t08, missing from the original design: base 0-shot code-500 was scored greedy (0/497, degeneracy 25.4%). If greedy also suppresses base accuracy, the 'no measurable code generation at 200M' conclusion rests partly on a decoder artifact. READ: base t=0.8 accuracy still 0/497 (or below 12.6%) => the capability conclusion is decoder-independent and stands. Above 12.6% => every base code number today measured the decoder and the conclusion is void.
- **Learned:** The control arm the original design was missing. Sampling halves degeneracy on the base model too and moves accuracy not at all -- it was already exactly zero and stays exactly zero. So the capability conclusion is decoder-independent: across two checkpoints and two temperatures, every code accuracy sits on the floor while the degeneracy rate moves by 13-39 points. Degeneracy and accuracy are separate channels and only the first responds to the decoder.
- **So:** '200M has no measurable code generation in this regime' is confirmed and no longer needs the decoder caveat. Report generative FORMAT metrics with their temperature; capability metrics do not need it.

### degen_t08 — degeneracy 55.8% -> 17.0% (n=500); accuracy 2.2% -> 1.0%; no-fence 87.8% -> 78.0%
- **Asked:** The 55.8% degenerate-repetition rate of SFT v2 (vs base 0-shot 24.7%, base 3-shot 6.6%) is produced by greedy decoding, not by the model. READ: degeneracy at t=0.8 below base's 24.7% => the loop is a decoding artifact and every greedy code-500 number today is measuring the decoder; at or above 55.8% => the model itself is non-terminating and the cause is upstream in the SFT data or recipe. Accuracy at t=0.8 is a different estimator than greedy and is NOT comparable to the 2.2%.
- **Learned:** The repetition loop is a greedy-decoding artifact: sampling at t=0.8 cuts degeneracy by two thirds, below base 0-shot's 25.4%. It hid nothing -- accuracy stayed on the floor and in fact fell (greedy is the better pass@1 estimator for a weak model). So the format-side numbers (fence rate, degeneracy) are properties of the decoder and must be reported with their temperature; the capability-side number (accuracy at the floor) is decoder-independent and now rests on firmer ground, having been suspected of being a decoder artifact and checked.
- **So:** Report every generative format metric with its temperature; do not build a repetition penalty (it would suppress a symptom that carries no capability signal); do not change the SFT recipe or the SFT pack on this evidence. The 200M-has-no-measurable-code-generation conclusion stands.

### fullmla_0.2b_0830 — train 3.204 val 3.428, 497s, 7 cards; score matrix: domain NLL 3.6749, minimal-pair 0.682, ceval 22.5% (z=-2.1)
- **Asked:** First 0830v1 budget point on KDA + full causal gated MLA + AttnRes Full. Does it train stably at batch 16 x accum 2, and what does the frozen b0 panel read on it? Open assumption, flagged before the run: web_hq is 32.4% of this mix and it is not yet confirmed whether its build applied the quality head or only the pass1-3 regex layer, whose measured recall against hand-read garbage is 3.4%.
- **Learned:** INVALID as a scaling point. The web_hq domain (32.4% of the mix) was silently repointed: data/corpus/web_hq/ became 8 symlinks into the in-progress CCI3 build at 22:24, and /data00/tokens_web_hq.pt was rebuilt at 22:25 from those symlinks, 16 minutes before this run started at 22:41. The run therefore trained on CCI3, the corpus barred from every mix until its quality head is retrained (measured filter recall 1.7%, hand-read junk 30-39%). The original fineweb2 web_hq shards are gone from /work and /data00, and fineweb2 is absent from data/raw, so the domain cannot be rebuilt from what is on the box.
- **So:** Point 0.2b does not enter the scaling curve. Architecture and run mechanics are validated (stable loss, no NaN, batch 16 x accum 2 fits at 50.8GB/card), so the run is kept as a mechanics check only. Before any budget point is rerun, web_hq must have a known provenance and corpus_fp_matches must cover every mix domain, not one.

### warmup_smoke_w3 — val 3.476 (train 3.471, min 3.193), 428s
- **Asked:** Floor 3: does the 0.52 val gap vs warmup=20 close? Ladder 2->3->5->8, first that lands within run noise of the control.
- **Learned:** monotonic: 2->3.658, 3->3.476, 20->3.135. Even 20 may not be the optimum but it is the chosen smaller-bias config
- **So:** ladder stopped at 3 per fb; 5/8 cancelled

### warmup_smoke_w20 — val 3.135 (train 3.126, min 2.865), 428s -- the control; best of the three
- **Asked:** Control for warmup_smoke: identical except warmup=20. Criterion 3 needs this: final val loss same magnitude as floor-2 (3.658).
- **Learned:** warmup=20 wins at every logged step vs floor 2 and 3: absolute warmup steps matter, not fraction
- **So:** six points use fixed warmup=20 (fb ruling)

### warmup_smoke — floor-2: val 3.658 (train 3.658, min 3.354), 428s; control warmup=20: val 3.135
- **Asked:** pure-1% warmup with floor 2 is stable at the 0.2b point: loss decreases over the first 20 steps with no spike, gnorm not persistently above clip 1.0, final val loss same magnitude as the warmup=20 control. If it fails, floor escalates 2->3->5->8.
- **Learned:** floor 2 did not blow up (loss monotonic, no NaN, gnorm controlled) but loses 0.52 val vs warmup=20 at every logged step -- short warmup damages early steps and the gap persists. Criterion 3 failed: warmup materially matters at 218 steps.
- **So:** escalate floor 2->3 per the pre-authorized ladder

### 0830v1_repeat4 — architecture superseded before scoring: 1024 sliding window deleted in b3cad87, AttnRes default changed blocks=4 -> Full
- **Asked:** Muennighoff 2305.16264 fits each repeat at ~94% of the previous one and reports 4 epochs costing ~0.5% val loss, measured at 7M-9B params on C4. Does it hold at 166M on this mix? Read the result against the fresh-data fit prediction at 13B (2.360). If repeating lands near it, 30B of new corpus buys ~0.075 nat over repeating what we have.
- **Learned:** old architecture (1024 sliding window + attn_res_blocks=4); checkpoints archived to pod retired_pre0830v2/
- **So:** not a baseline; kept only as lessons-44 E2 analysis input (domain_loss.py plots all six points in one coordinate system, zero retrain cost)
